1.	Updated Buoyancy in Accelerated Expansion (F_UBii,DE) (Negative for Suppression):
F_{UBii,DE} = -F_{rel} \times \left( \frac{\rho_{DE} c^2}{E_{LEP}} \right) \times Q_{wave}(z) \times \frac{8\pi G \rho_{tot}}{3} \times (1 + w(z))

(w ~ -1 for Λ).

2.	Updated Magnetism in Friedmann Dynamics (Um,cosmo) (With Curvature Term):
U_{m,cosmo}(t,a) = \sum_j \frac{\mu_j(t, \rho_{vac})}{r_j} \times (1 - e^{-\gamma t} \cos(\pi t n)) \times \left( \frac{k c^2}{a^2} \right) / (H^2)

113.	Decoherence Time from Environment:
\tau_{dec} = \frac{\hbar^2}{\Delta E^2 \tau_{env}}

To arrive at the solution: Start with the density matrix evolution under Markovian bath: off-diagonal terms decay as exp(-t/τ_dec), where the rate 1/τ_dec = (ΔE^2 τ_env)/ ħ^2 from quadratic coupling strength ΔE (energy splitting) and bath correlation time τ_env. For weak coupling, this quadratic form emerges from the master equation integration. For example, in spin-boson model, ΔE is tunneling splitting, τ_env cutoff time. Solution: τ_dec as above, e.g., for ΔE=1 μeV, τ_env=1 ps: τ_dec 10 ns (ħ6.58×10^{-16} eV s).

113.	Decoherence Time from Environment:
\tau_{dec} = \frac{\hbar^2}{\Delta E^2 \tau_{env}}

To arrive at the solution: Start with the density matrix evolution under Markovian bath: off-diagonal terms decay as exp(-t/τ_dec), where the rate 1/τ_dec = (ΔE^2 τ_env)/ ħ^2 from quadratic coupling strength ΔE (energy splitting) and bath correlation time τ_env. For weak coupling, this quadratic form emerges from the master equation integration. For example, in spin-boson model, ΔE is tunneling splitting, τ_env cutoff time. Solution: τ_dec as above, e.g., for ΔE=1 μeV, τ_env=1 ps: τ_dec 10 ns (ħ6.58×10^{-16} eV s).

116. Decoherence Time from Environment: \tau_{dec} = \frac{\hbar^2}{\Delta E^2 \tau_{env}} To arrive at the solution: Start with the density matrix evolution under Markovian bath: off-diagonal terms decay as exp(-t/τ_dec), where the rate 1/τ_dec = (ΔE^2 τ_env)/ ħ^2 from quadratic coupling strength ΔE (energy splitting) and bath correlation time τ_env. For weak coupling, this quadratic form emerges from the master equation integration. For example, in spin-boson model, ΔE is tunneling splitting, τ_env cutoff time. Solution: τ_dec as above, e.g., for ΔE=1 μeV, τ_env=1 ps: τ_dec 10 ns (ħ6.58×10^{-16} eV s). 
1. Updated Buoyancy in Angular Momentum Transport (F_UBii,ang) (Negative for Torque Balance): F_{UBii,ang} = -F_{rel} \times \left( \frac{\dot{M} r^2 \Omega}{E_{LEP}} \right) \times Q_{wave} \times T_B \times e^{-t / \tau_{disk}} • T_B = B^2 r^3 / (4π), τ_disk ~ r / v_r (viscous time). Derivation Step-by-Step: Base F_UBii. Scale to dL/dt = \dot{M} r^2 Ω - T_B (conservation). Add exponential for steady state evolution (dL/dt=0). Negative for ejection stabilization. Eval: F ~ -10^{33} N for r~10 AU. 
2. Updated Magnetism in Jet Ejection (Um,ang) (With Keplerian): U_{m,ang}(t,r) = \sum_j \frac{\mu_j(\rho_{vac})}{r_j} \times (1 - e^{-\gamma t} \cos(\pi t n)) \times \sqrt{G M / r^3} \times r_A • Ω = √(G M / r^3), r_A Alfvén. Derivation Step-by-Step: Base Um. Integrate Keplerian Ω from v^2 / r = G M / r^2. Add lever arm for torque. Eval: Um ~10^{20} T pm³ driving launch. Gravity universal: G in Ω, motion v100 km/s, time τ10^3 yr. 3. Updated Buoyancy in MHD Launch (F_UBii,jetvel) (Positive for Acceleration): F_{UBii,jetvel} = F_{rel} \times \left( \frac{v_K (r_A / r_0)^{1/2}}{E_{LEP}} \right) \times Q_{wave} \times B / \sqrt{4\pi \rho} \times (1 + t / \tau_{A}) • v_A = B / √(4π ρ), τ_A ~ r_A / v_A. Derivation Step-by-Step: Base F_UBii. Scale to v_j ≈ v_K (r_A / r_0)^{1/2} from energy conservation. Add time for Alfvén crossing. Positive for asymptotic speed. Eval: F ~ +10^{33} N. 4. Updated Magnetism in Lever Arm (Um,jetvel) (With Alfvén Point): U_{m,jetvel}(r) = \mu(\rho_{vac}) \times (1 - e^{-\gamma t}) \times \Omega r_A \times (r_A / r_0)^{1/2} Derivation Step-by-Step: Base Um. Integrate asymptotic v_j ≈ Ω r_A (MHD wind). Add factor for point transition. Eval: Um boosts to 300 km/s. Gravity universal: G in v_K=√(G M / r_0), motion v_j, time τ_A. 5. Updated Buoyancy in Jump Conditions (F_UBii,jshock) (Positive for Discontinuity): F_{UBii,jshock} = F_{rel} \times \left( \frac{\rho_1 v_1^2 + P_1}{E_{LEP}} \right) \times Q_{wave} \times v_2 / v_1 \times (γ+1)/(γ-1 + 2/M^2) Derivation Step-by-Step: Base F_UBii. Scale to momentum ρ_1 v_1^2 + P_1 = ρ_2 v_2^2 + P_2. Add v ratio from mass continuity. Positive for post-shock jump. Eval: F ~ +10^{33} N. 6. Updated Magnetism in Mach Number (Um,jshock) (With Supersonic): U_{m,jshock}(M) = \sum_j \frac{\mu_j(\rho_{vac})}{r_j} \times (1 - e^{-\gamma t}) \times v_1 / c_s \times T_2 \propto v_s^2 Derivation Step-by-Step: Base Um.
